  832: async function verifySlotPersistedViaBackend({ date, slotHHMM }) {
  833:   try {
  834:     const base = PITACO_API_BASE.replace(/\/+$/, "");
  835:     const u = `${base}/api/pitaco/results?date=${encodeURIComponent(date)}&lottery=${encodeURIComponent(LOTTERY)}`;

  839:       validateStatus: (s) => s >= 200 && s < 400,

  841:     });

  844:     if (!j || typeof j !== "object") return { ok: false, reason: "BAD_JSON" };

  848:     if (foundInDraws) return { ok: true, where: "draws", drawsCount: draws.length };

  851:     const hh = hhFromSlotHHMM(slotHHMM);
  852: 
  853:     const foundInSlots = slots.some((s) => {
  854:       const v = (s && (s.hour || s.close_hour || s.closeHour || s.close || s.slot || s.time || s.close_hour_raw)) ?? "";
  855:       const t = String(v).trim();
  856:       return t.startsWith(hh);
  857:     });
  858: 
  859:     if (foundInSlots) return { ok: true, where: "slots", slotsCount: slots.length };
  860: 
  861:     return { ok: false, reason: "NOT_FOUND", drawsCount: draws.length, slotsCount: slots.length };
  862:   } catch (e) {
  863:     const code = e?.code || e?.cause?.code || "";
  864:     const msg = e?.message || e?.cause?.message || String(e);
  865:     const r = code ? `${code}:${msg}` : msg;
  866:     return { ok: false, reason: `EX:${r}` };
  867:   }
  868: }

  191:   const _fetch = await getFetch();
  192: 
  193:   const TIMEOUT_MS = envInt("FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS", 15000);
  194:   const RETRIES = envInt("FETCH_RETRIES", 2);
  195:   const DELAY_MS = envInt("FETCH_RETRY_DELAY_MS", 600);
  196: 
  197:   const maxAttempts = 1 + Math.max(0, RETRIES);
  198: 
  199:   for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= maxAttempts; attempt++) {
  200:     const ctrl =
  201:       typeof AbortController !== "undefined" ? new AbortController() : null;
  202:     const t = ctrl ? setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), TIMEOUT_MS) : null;
  203: 
  204:     try {
  205:       const r = await _fetch(url, {
  206:         headers: { accept: "application/json" },
  207:         signal: ctrl ? ctrl.signal : undefined,
  208:       });
  209: 
  210:       const txt = await r.text();
  211:       let j = null;
  212:       try {
  213:         j = JSON.parse(txt);
  214:       } catch {}
  215: 
  216:       if (!r.ok) {
  217:         const msg = j && j.error ? j.error : txt || `HTTP ${r.status}`;
  218:         throw new Error(msg);
  219:       }
  220: 
  221:       // ✅ IMPORTANT: API pode responder 200 com ok:false
  222:       if (j && j.ok === false) {
  223:         const msg = j.error || j.message || "Resposta ok:false";
  224:         throw new Error(msg);
  225:       }
  226: 
  227:       return j;
  228:     } catch (e) {
  229:       const isLast = attempt === maxAttempts;
  230:       const msg =
  231:         e?.name === "AbortError"
  232:           ? `timeout após ${TIMEOUT_MS}ms`
  233:           : e?.message || String(e);
  234: 
  235:       if (isLast) throw new Error(`${msg} (url=${url})`);
  236: 
  237:       // retry
  238:       // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
  239:       console.warn(
  240:         `[WARN] fetch falhou (${attempt}/${maxAttempts}) url=${url} => ${msg}`
  241:       );
  242:       if (DELAY_MS > 0) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, DELAY_MS));
  243:     } finally {
  244:       if (t) clearTimeout(t);

  396: function normalizeImgSrc(src) {
  397:   const s = safeStr(src);
  398:   if (!s) return "";
  399: 
  400:   if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(s)) return s;
  401: 
  402:   const base = publicBase();
  403: 
  404:   if (s.startsWith("/")) return `${base}${s}`;
  405:   if (s.startsWith("public/")) return `${base}/${s.slice("public/".length)}`;
  406:   if (s.startsWith("img/")) return `${base}/${s}`;
  407: 
  408:   return `${base}/${s}`;
  409: }

  287: 
  288: function normalizeImgSrc(src) {
  289:   const s = safeStr(src);
  290:   if (!s) return "";
  291: 
  292:   if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(s)) return s;
  293: 
  294:   const base = publicBase();
  295: 
  296:   if (s.startsWith("/")) return `${base}${s}`;
  297:   if (s.startsWith("public/")) return `${base}/${s.slice("public/".length)}`;
  298:   if (s.startsWith("img/")) return `${base}/${s}`;
  299: 
  300:   return `${base}/${s}`;
  301: }
  302:

  316:
